Remove thousands separator (.)\n    # Replace decimal comma with .\n    if ',' in s:\n        # European format\n        s = s.replace('.', '')\n        s = s.replace(',', '.')\n    return float(s)\n\ndef parse_us_number(s):\n    return float(s.strip())\n\ndef parse_timestamp(s, fmt='iso'):\n    s = s.strip()\n    if fmt == 'euro':\n        # DD.MM.YYYY HH:MM:SS\n        return datetime.strptime(s, '%d.%m.%Y %H:%M:%S')\n    else:\n        # YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS\n        return datetime.strptime(s, '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')\n\ndef is_valid_timestamp(s):\n    s = s.strip()\n    # Try ISO format\n    try:\n        datetime.strptime(s, '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')\n        return True\n    except ValueError:\n        pass\n    try:\n        datetime.strptime(s, '%d.%m.%Y %H:%M:%S')\n        return True\n    except ValueError:\n        pass\n    return False\n\ndef convert_to_kwh(value, unit, reading_type):\n    \"\"\"Convert a single reading value to kWh\"\"\"\n    unit = unit.lower().strip()\n    if reading_type == 'power':\n        # Power reading in kW -> energy in kWh over 15 minutes\n        return value * 0.25\n    else:  # energy\n        if unit in ('kwh', 'kw-h', 'kwh '):\n            return value\n        elif unit in ('mwh', 'mw-h'):\n            return value * 1000\n        elif unit in ('wh', 'w-h', 'wh ', 'w-h '):\n            return value / 1000.0\n        elif unit in ('btu', 'btu '):\n            # 1 BTU = 1055.06 J, 1 kWh = 3.6e6 J\n            return value * 1055.06 / 3_600_000.0\n        elif unit in ('kw', 'kw '):  # Should be handled by reading_type\n            return value * 0.25\n        else:\n            return value  # Unknown unit, try as-is\n\n# Process each farm\nresults = {}\nfor farm_id in sorted(id_to_timezone.keys()):\n    # Get calibration info\n    farm_cal = cal_data[farm_id]\n    calibration_offset = farm_cal['calibration_data']['calibration_offset']\n    value_column = farm_cal['calibration_data']['value_column_name']\n    reading_type = farm_cal['sensor_info']['reading_type']\n    unit = farm_cal['sensor_info']['unit']\n    timezone_str = id_to_timezone[farm_id]\n    tz = pytz.timezone(timezone_str)\n    \n    # Build filepath\n    filepath = f'/app/dataset/production_{farm_id}.csv'\n    \n    try:\n        # Detect encoding\n        encoding = detect_encoding(filepath)\n        \n        # Read raw lines to detect delimiter\n        with open(filepath, 'r', encoding=encoding) as f:\n            lines = f.readlines()\n        \n        # Find the header line (first line with 'timestamp')\n        header_line = None\n        data_start = 0\n        for i, line in enumerate(lines):\n            if line.startswith('timestamp') or line.startswith('\\x00t\\x00i\\x00m\\x00e\\x00s\\x00t\\x00a\\x00m\\x00p'):\n                header_line = line\n                data_start = i + 1\n                break\n        \n        if header_line is None:\n            results[farm_id] = 0\n            continue\n        \n        # Detect delimiter\n        if ';' in header_line:\n            delimiter = ';'\n            euro_format = True\n        else:\n            delimiter = ','\n            euro_format = False\n        \n        # Find the column index for the value column\n        # Clean up the header for parsing (remove null bytes and split)\n        clean_header = header_line.replace('\\x00', '').strip()\n        columns = clean_header.split(delimiter)\n        columns = [c.strip() for c in columns]\n        \n        # Find the value column - try exact match first (with trailing spaces)\n        value_col_name = value_column\n        col_idx = None\n        for idx, col in enumerate(columns):\n            if col == value_col_name:\n                col_idx = idx\n                break\n        \n        if col_idx is None:\n            # Try stripped match\n            for idx, col in enumerate(columns):\n                if col.strip() == value_col_name.strip():\n                    col_idx = idx\n                    break\n        \n        if col_idx is None:\n            results[farm_id] = 0\n            continue\n        \n        # Parse data\n        data = []\n        timestamps_local = []\n        inverter_temps = []\n        \n        for line in lines[data_start:]:\n            line = line.strip()\n            if not line:\n                continue\n            \n            # Remove null bytes\n            clean_line = line.replace('\\x00', '')\n            \n            # Split into fields\n            fields = clean_line.split(delimiter)\n            fields = [f.strip() for f in fields]\n            \n            if len(fields) <= col_idx:\n                continue\n            \n            # Parse timestamp\n            ts_str = fields[0]\n            if not is_valid_timestamp(ts_str):\n                continue\n            \n            # Get value\n            val_str = fields[col_idx]\n            if val_str in ('', 'null', 'N/A', 'timeout', 'offline', 'ERR'):\n                continue\n            \n            # Parse number\n            try:\n                if euro_format:\n                    value = parse_euro_number(val_str)\n                else:\n                    value = parse_us_number(val_str)\n            except (ValueError, TypeError):\n                continue\n            \n            # Get inverter temperature (column index 1)\n            try:\n                if euro_format:\n                    inv_temp = parse_euro_number(fields[1])\n                else:\n                    inv_temp = parse_us_number(fields[1])\n            except (ValueError, TypeError):\n                inv_temp = None\n            \n            # Parse timestamp\n            try:\n                if '.' in ts_str and not ts_str.startswith('20'):  # European format\n                    ts = datetime.strptime(ts_str, '%d.%m.%Y %H:%M:%S')\n                else:\n                    ts = datetime.strptime(ts_str, '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')\n            except ValueError:\n                continue\n            \n            data.append({\n                'timestamp': ts,\n                'value': value,\n                'inverter_temp': inv_temp\n            })\n        \n        if not data:\n            results[farm_id] = 0\n            continue\n        \n        # If inverter_temperature_C > 85.0, invalidate this reading and next 45 minutes\n        if 'inverter_temp' in df.columns:\n            # Find rows with temp > 85\n            hot_indices = df[df['inverter_temp'] > 85.0].index\n            invalid_indices = set()\n            for idx in hot_indices:\n                # Invalidate this reading and next 3 (45 min = 3 * 15 min)\n                for offset in range(4):\n                    invalid_idx = idx + offset\n                    if invalid_idx < len(df):\n                        invalid_indices.add(invalid_idx)\n            df = df.drop(list(invalid_indices), errors='ignore')\n        \n        # Handle duplicates\n        df = df.drop_duplicates(subset=['timestamp'])\n        \n        # Convert to UTC\n        df['utc_time'] = df['timestamp'].apply(lambda t: tz.localize(t).astimezone(pytz.UTC))\n        \n        # Convert value to kWh\n        df['kwh_value'] = df['true_value'].apply(\n            lambda v: convert_to_kwh(v, unit, reading_type)\n        )\n        \n        # Filter to UTC day\n        df = df[(df['utc_time'] >= utc_start) & (df['utc_time'] < utc_end)]\n        \n        # Handle maintenance windows (in local time)\n        if farm_id == 'FARM_C':\n            # Firmware update on March 16 08:00-12:00 JST - COMPLETED\n            maint_start = datetime(2024, 3, 16, 8, 0, 0)\n            maint_end = datetime(2024, 3, 16, 12, 0, 0)\n            df = df[~((df['timestamp'] >= maint_start) & (df['timestamp'] < maint_end))]\n        elif farm_id == 'FARM_F':\n            # Cleaning on March 15 12:00-14:00 CET - COMPLETED\n            maint_start = datetime(2024, 3, 15, 12, 0, 0)\n            maint_end = datetime(2024, 3, 15, 14, 0, 0)\n            df = df[~((df['timestamp'] >= maint_start) & (df['timestamp'] < maint_end))]\n        \n        total = round(df['kwh_value'].sum())\n        results[farm_id] = total\n        \n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error processing {farm_id}: {e}\")\n        results[farm_id] = 0\n\nreturn results\n```\n\nWait, I need to check all 24 farm IDs.
